LP step details:
Given a (length n) normalized sum S0 = sqrt(2n) perhaps. M = max conv(a,a). Constraint a * g ≤ M (full convolution length 2n-1), g≥0. Maximize sum g. This is a linear program. The optimal g has maybe sparse support. Then normalize g to sum S0 and update a = (1-t)a + t g. They claim M nonincreasing. If we can run many iterations with a small LP, we can converge to a local optimum. Need solve LPs fast.

For n=3809, variables n, constraints 2n-1≈7617. Dense matrix 7617*3809 ~29M doubles ~232MB, maybe too high but maybe okay? But plus solver workspace huge. Need sparse? scipy.optimize.linprog can handle sparse? It uses HiGHS which supports sparse? We can provide sparse matrix (scipy.sparse) maybe. But constructing banded sparse with 3n nonzeros? The convolution constraints: (a*g)_k =∑_{j} a_j g_{k-j} (with bounds). For each g_j, coefficients a_j in k from j to j+n-1. Matrix band width n: each column has n nonzeros, total ~n^2/2? Actually dense-ish: each row has up to n nonzeros, total ≈n^2 (not banded? It's Toeplitz lower triangular with width n). For n=3809, ~14.5M nonzeros > memory ~116MB plus overhead huge. Too big for linprog. Need smaller n for LP. We can choose n=e.g., 512 or 1024, LP feasible. Score target maybe achievable with n=1024? If inf large-n ~1.5, maybe 1024 enough? We can test maybe not. Subgradient descent on simplex:

FFT score:
```
def fft_convolve(a):
    n=a.size; m=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)[:2*n-1]
    return b
```
For n=3809, m=16384, complex arrays ~256KB, fine. For small many, use cached sizes? We can map n to m.

For LSE surrogate and gradient, use active? We can first use Nelder-Mead over Fourier params with exact/fft max. Gradient not needed.

Fourier parameterization details:
- Choose n. Let t=np.arange(n)/n.
- Basis: modes m=1..R with frequencies f_m (integers maybe 1,2,3,...). p(t)=softplus(c + Σ A_m cos(2π f_m i/n + φ_m)) / sum. Use cos/sin to allow phase; represent as pairs (amplitude m, frequency, phase). We can precompute basis matrix X (n x 2R) with sin/cos. Params: intercept c (maybe fixed 0) and 2R coefficients linear. p = exp(c+Xθ) / sum. This ensures nonneg and smooth. Alternatively p = normalize(base + Xθ clipped to eps); softplus(exp) may limit shape. Use exp to allow wide dynamic. But if θ large, p peaked; could have high score. Use bounded softplus? We can clip p after normalization to [0,1] always. For linear combination, negative values clipped to 0 creates non-differentiability but okay for gradient-free.
- Maybe use `p = (base + Xθ); p=np.clip(p,0,None); p/=sum`. Base maybe 1. This allows zeros and sharp features if coefficients negative. Use base 1 to keep nonnegative; parameters can reduce some entries to 0.
- Need sum of cos/sin zero if frequencies integer and n multiple? If n not multiple, sum modes not exactly zero; but normalization handles.
- We can random initialize θ with amplitudes in [-0.8,0.8]. Multi-start.

Search over θ:
- Use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with method='Nelder-Mead' (no grad, handles non-smooth) over θ length 2R+1. Set maxiter, xatol fatol. Objective: `score_fft(p(θ))` maybe add small regularization? Nelder-Mead can be expensive: each iteration 2D+1 evals; D=20, 1000 iter=20k evals. FFT each 0.01s =>200s per start. Too much. We can reduce D=8 (freq 1-4 with sin/cos) and maxiter 200. Or use coordinate ascent.
- Better: Use `scipy.optimize.differential_evolution` too heavy.
- Use random hill-climbing: for each trial, start random θ; for iterations, perturb a few coords randomly, evaluate, accept if better or with simulated annealing. D=10, 1000 evals/start.
- Since final code cannot tune, maybe simpler: generate many random θ (e.g., 5000) and evaluate FFT score; keep best; then local coordinate search on best. Random search in D=10 may not find target.

Let's implement a simple multistart hill-climbing with random restarts:
```
def optimize_pattern(q, K, n_starts, iters, time):
  best=None
  for start in range(n_starts):
    if start==0: θ=zeros
    elif start==1: if parent available resample to q by averaging
    else θ=rng.normal(size=q)*scale
    h=softmax(θ); p=tile(h,K)/K
    cur=score_fft(p)
    step=0.1
    for it in range(iters):
      # perturb random coords (or all small Langevin)
      θ_new = θ + rng.normal(0, step, size=q)
      # keep bound? softmax handles
      p=...
      s=score_fft(p)
      if s<cur: θ=θ_new; cur=s
      else: sometimes step*=0.8; no accept
      if check time break
    update best
```

LP iteration implementation details:
```
def lp_refine(a, time_limit, max_iters):
    n=a.size
    S=math.sqrt(2*n)
    # a normalized sum S: a = p*S
    M=score_p(a/S) # current score = max conv a
    best_p=a/S; best_score=M
    for iter:
       # solve maybe 3-5 LPs with random tilt
       for tilt in range(3):
           c = -np.ones(n)
           if tilt>0: c += 1e-3*rng.normal(n)
           A=build_conv_matrix(a/S? wait constraints use a=S*p; scale cancels? Use normalized a=S*p. M=score_p.
           res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':20})
           if res.status not in [0,3?] maybe skip. g=res.x
           L=g.sum()
           g_norm = g*S/max(L,eps) if L>0
           # line search t list [0.01..0.5]
           for t in ts: p_try=(1-t)*(a/S)+t*g_norm
              score=score_fft(p_try)
              if score<best_score-1e-12: update a=S*p_try, M=score, break
       if no improvement: random perturb best_p, recompute a, continue or break after several stagnant.
```

```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s
```

Score:
```
def direct_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    p=a/a.sum()
    b=np.convolve(p,p)
    return float(2*n*b.max())
```
But for internal many, avoid re-normalize? Use p prenormalized.
```
def conv_max_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    m=1
    while m < 2*n-1: m <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)[:2*n-1]
    return float(b.max())
def score_p(p):
   n=p.size; return 2*n*conv_max_fft(p)
```
FFT cost repeated for same n. We can cache m in dict.
For lace small N, direct conv max may be faster and exact:
```
def score_fft_or_direct(p):
   if p.size<128: return 2*np.convolve(p,p).max()*p.size
   else: FFT
```
But direct `np.convolve` returns length2n-1; max. Use for N≤256.

LSE surrogate:

def score_lse_fft(p,beta):
   n=p.size; ... b fft; z=beta*b; zmax=z.max(); return (zmax + log(sum(exp(z-zmax))))/beta
```
If beta<=0 return score.

def softmax(x):

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _fft_len(n):
    m=1
    target=2*n-1
    while m<target: m <<=1
    return m

_fft_cache={}
def fft_len(n):
   m=_fft_cache.get(n)
   if m is None: ...; _fft_cache[n]=m
   return m

def conv_fft_max(p):
   n=p.size; m=...; fa=np.fft.rfft(p,m); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, m)[:2*n-1]
   return b
```
For score only max, but for subgradient need full b. Function returns b; score can max.
```
def conv_full_fft(p):
   n=p.size; m=...; fa=np.fft.rfft(p,m); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)[:2*n-1]; return b
def score_fft(p): return 2*len(p)*float(np.max(conv_full_fft(p)))
def lse_score_fft(p,beta):
   b=conv_full_fft(p);
   if beta<=0: return score_fft
   z=beta*b; z-=z.max(); return float(z.max()/beta + math.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))/beta)
```
Careful: if we modify z in place, z max changed. Use `zm=np.max(z); return float((zm + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))/beta)`.

def conv_full(p):
   if p.size<128: return np.convolve(p,p)
   return conv_full_fft(p)
def score(p): return 2*n*np.max(conv_full(p))
```

def pattern_seq(theta,q,K):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    if theta.size!=q or not finite: return None
    h=softmax(theta)
    p=np.tile(h,K)/K
    return p

def search_pattern(q,K,time_limit,rng):
    if time_left <30 or q*K<32: return
    beta = 1500.0
    def obj(theta):
       p=pattern_seq(theta,q,K)
       if p is None: return 1e100
       return lse_score_fft(p,beta)
    def exact_obj(theta): ...
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(q))
    # parent resample
    par=get parent maybe
    if par is not None: starts.append(resample(par,q))
    for phase in np.linspace(0,1, q//4? no):
       t=np.arange(q)/q
       starts.append(0.6*np.cos(2*np.pi*t + phase))
       starts.append(0.6*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + phase))
    for _ in range(3): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.8,q))
    for x0 in starts:
       if time_left <30: break
       try:
          res=minimize(obj, x0, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':150, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-8})
          if res.x is not None: consider(res.x)
       except: pass
```

```
def local_p_search(p, time_limit, rng):
    n=p.size; if n<32 or n>8192: return p
    p=normalize(p); best=p; best_s=score_fft(p); b=conv_full_fft(p)
    step=0.05
    for iters while time:
       mode=rng.integers(4)
       if mode==0: # log pert
           theta=np.log(best+1e-12)
           theta += step*rng.normal(n)
           cand=softmax(theta)
       elif mode==1: # block mult
           cand=best.copy()
           width=int(np.exp(rng.normal(math.log(max(2,n//64)), math.log(4)))) clip
           start=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
           factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25))
           cand[start:start+width]*=factor
           if rng.random()<0.3: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
       elif mode==2: # subgradient using b
           k=int(np.argmax(b)); d=...
           for eta in [0.01,0.05,0.1]: cand=best - eta*(d-d.mean()); cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
           choose first finite; maybe normalize
       else: # random Fourier band
           t=np.arange(n)/n
           m1=rng.integers(1,min(8,n//4)); m2=rng.integers(1,min(8,n//4))
           phase1=rng.uniform(0,2π); ...
           mod=1+0.1*(np.cos(2πm1t+phase1)+np.cos(2πm2t+phase2))
           cand=best*mod
       cand=normalize(cand)
       s=score_fft(cand)
       if s<best_s: update; b=conv_full_fft(cand); step=min(step*1.03,0.5)
       else: step=max(step*0.95,0.01)
```

Pattern search (after DE). Define parent resample:
```
def resample(arr, q):
   if arr is None or arr.size==0: return zeros(q)
   idx=np.linspace(0, arr.size-1, q).astype(int)
   vals=arr[idx]
   vals=np.clip(vals,0,None); s=vals.sum(); if s<=0: return zeros; return vals/s
```
Because p sum1, sampling q points not representative of integral; better average over windows:
```
q segments of arr: start=floor(i*N/q), stop=floor((i+1)*N/q); vals=arr[start:stop].mean(/?); normalize.
```
Use average over segments, gives shape.
```
def segment_avg(arr,q): vals=[arr[i*N//q:(i+1)*N//q].mean() ...]
```
Good.

def lp_refine(p0, time_limit, max_iters=20):
    n=p0.size; S=math.sqrt(2*n)
    a=p0*S
    best_p=p0.copy(); best_s=score_fast(best_p)
    M=float(best_s)
    no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem<30 or M? break
       improved=False
       for tilt in range(2):
           if time_left()<20: break
           c=-np.ones(n)
           if tilt: c += 1e-4*rng.normal(n)
           A=build_conv_matrix(a)
           try:
              res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=M,bounds=(0,None),method='highs',options={'time_limit':min(20, rem/2)})
           except Exception: continue
           if res.status not in (0,3) or res.x is None: continue
           g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.clip(g,0,None)
           L=g.sum()
           if L<=0: continue
           g_norm=g*S/L
           # if extreme, maybe blend with a/S
           for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75):
              pn=(1-t)*(a/S) + t*g_norm
              pn=np.clip(pn,0,None); ssum=pn.sum(); if ssum<=0: continue
              pn/=ssum
              s=score_fast(pn)
              if s < best_s - 1e-12:
                  best_p=pn; best_s=s; a=pn*S; M=s; improved=True; break
           if improved: no_improve=0; break
       if not improved:
           no_improve+=1
           # random perturb
           p2=normalize(best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))
           s2=score_fast(p2)
           if s2<best_s: best_p=p2; best_s=s2; a=best_p*S; M=s2; no_improve=0
           elif no_improve>5: break
    return best_p,best_s
```

_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}
def _next_fft_len(n):
    target=2*n-1
    res=_FFT_LEN_CACHE.get(n)
    if res is None:
       m=1
       while m<target: m <<=1
       res=m; _FFT_LEN_CACHE[n]=m
    return res

def conv_full(p):
    n=p.size

        return np.convolve(p,p)

- `score_fast(p)`:
```
def score_fast(p):
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return inf
    p=p/s
    b=conv_full(p)
    return float(2*n*np.max(b))
```
But if p already normalized and finite, repeated sum okay. For speed, avoid in tight? Fine.
- `lse_score(p,beta)`:

b=conv_full(p); z=beta*b; zm=np.max(z); z=z-zm; return float((zm + math.log(np.sum(np.exp(z))))/beta)
```
If beta=0 return score.
- `softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.size==0 or not np.isfinite(x).all(): return None
    x=x-x.max()
    e=np.exp(x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return e/s
```

return a/s
```

Then grad_θ: for p=softmax(θ), dp_j/dθ_l = p_j (X? Wait θ are log h? For pattern p=tile(softmax(θ),K)/K. h=softmax(θ). p_i = h_{i mod q}/K. Derivative wrt θ_l = (1/K) dp_h_l? Actually p_i depends on h_i: ∂h_j/∂θ_l = h_j(δ_{jl} - h_l). p_i = h_{i mod q}/K, so ∂p_i/∂θ_l = (1/K) h_{r}(...). Then grad_θ_l = ∑_i grad_p_i ∂p_i/∂θ_l = (1/K)* [ ∑_r across reps ∑ grad_{r+rep} ...]. We can aggregate `g_h = np.array([np.sum(grad_p[i*q:(i+1)*q]) for i in range(q)]) / K`? More precisely ∂p_i/∂θ_l = (1/K) h_r (δ_{r,l} - h_l). Sum_i grad_p_i ∂p_i = (1/K)[ h_l ∑_i grad_p_i - ∑_r h_r∑_i grad_{i mod q=r} ]. Let `G_r = ∑_{i mod q=r} grad_p_i`. Then grad_θ_l = (1/K)( h_l Psum - G_l ), where Psum=∑_i grad_p_i. This is efficient.

Pattern LSE objective with gradient:
```
def pattern_lse(theta, q, K, beta, cache={}):
   h=softmax(theta)
   if h None: return inf, zeros
   p=np.tile(h,K)/K
   n=p.size
   b=conv_full(p)
   # stable logsumexp and top weights
   z=beta*b
   zm=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-zm); wsum=w.sum()
   val=(zm + math.log(wsum))/beta
   # top K
   Ktop=min(64, n if n<=1024 else 128)
   if wsum>0:
      top=np.argpartition(w, -Ktop)[-Ktop:] if Ktop < b.size else np.arange(b.size)
      sub=w[top]; sub=sum? normalize: u=sub/sub.sum()
      d=np.zeros(n)
      for k,uval in zip(top,u):
         if uval<=0: continue
         lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
         idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
         src=k-idx
         d[idx] += 2.0*uval*p[src]
      Psum=d.sum()
      G=np.zeros(q)
      # aggregate: for r in 0..q-1, sum d[r:r:n]? Since p tile, p order repeat h K times: indices r + t*q? Wait np.tile(h,K) => [h0..h_{q-1}, h0..] period q. So residue r positions are r::q. Yes.
      G=d.reshape(K,q).sum(axis=0)? p length = q*K exactly if K integer. If pattern_seq length q*K, yes. d.reshape(K,q) (if C-order) rows each period. Sum axis0 -> per r. Good.
      grad_theta=(d.mean? Psum/K*h - G/K?) Formula: grad_θ_l=(1/K)(h_l*Psum - G_l).
      grad=(h*Psum - G)/K
      # gradient of objective wrt θ? We used d=grad of LSE wrt p? LSE derivative wrt p is sum u db/dp = d. Then chain as above. Yes d is ∂LSE/∂p. But objective is LSE, not multiplied. Good.
   return val, grad
```
Check chain: p_i = h_r/K. ∂LSE/∂θ_l = ∑_r (∂p_{r+rep}/∂θ_l) d_{r+rep}. ∂h_r/∂θ_l=h_r(δ_{rl}-h_l). Sum over reps K: K * (h_l Psum/K? Wait d_{r+rep} sum over reps = G_r. ∑_r [h_r(δ_{rl}-h_l)]/K * G_r = (1/K)(h_l G_l - h_l ∑_r G_r) = (h_l Psum - G_l)/K. Correct.
If N not exactly q*K? pattern_seq uses K int, yes.

Let's write code for `pattern_lse` carefully with FP:
```
def pattern_lse(theta,q,K,beta,top=128):
    h=softmax(theta)
    if h is None: return 1e100, np.zeros(q)
    p=np.empty(q*K)
    p[:] = np.tile(h,K)/K
    n=p.size
    b=conv_full(p)
    if b.size==0: return inf, zeros
    z=beta*b
    zm=np.max(z)
    w=np.exp(z-zm)
    wsum=np.sum(w)
    if wsum<=0 or not finite: return inf,zeros
    val=(zm + math.log(wsum))/beta
    topn=min(top, b.size)
    if topn < b.size:
       top_idx=np.argpartition(w, -topn)[-topn:]
    else: top_idx=np.arange(b.size)
    wtop=w[top_idx]; sw=wtop.sum()
    if sw<=0: return val, zeros
    u=wtop/sw
    d=np.zeros(n)
    for k,uval in zip(top_idx,u):
       if uval<=1e-15: continue
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo>hi: continue
       idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       src=k-idx
       d[idx] += (2.0*uval)*p[src]
    Psum=d.sum()
    if Psum>0 or n>0:
       G=d.reshape(K,q).sum(axis=0)  # d length q*K; if C-order and p tile [h0..,h0..], reshape(K,q) rows periods; sum axis0 gives per h position.
       grad=(h*Psum - G)/K
       grad -= grad.mean()
    else: grad=zeros
    return float(val), grad
```
Check `d.reshape(K,q)`: For q=4,K=2, d=[g00,g01,g02,g03,g10,g11,g12,g13] corresponding p [h0,h1,h2,h3,h0,h1,h2,h3]. Row0 [h0,h1,h2,h3], row1 [h0,h1,h2,h3]. Sum axis0 -> [g00+g10, ...], correct.
If K large, topn 128, loops 128. Good.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import minimize
        linprog = None
    except Exception:
        minimize = None
        linprog = None

_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}


def _next_fft_len(n):
    res = _FFT_LEN_CACHE.get(n)
    if res is None:
        target = max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1)
        m = 1
        while m < target:
            m <<= 1
        res = m
        _FFT_LEN_CACHE[int(n)] = res
    return res


def _normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
        if a.size == 0:
            return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _exact_score_arr(a):
    p = _normalize_sequence(a)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(p, p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2 * p.size * float(np.max(b)))


def _conv_full(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 128:
        return np.convolve(p, p)
    m = _next_fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    if b.size and float(b.min()) < 0.0:
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_fast(p):
    p = _normalize_sequence(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    b = _conv_full(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2 * p.size * float(np.max(b)))


def _lse_score(p, beta):
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return _score_fast(p)
    b = _conv_full(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    z = beta * b
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zm
    sm = math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(z))))
    return float((zm + sm) / beta)


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(x).all():
        return None
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return e / s


def _segment_average(arr, q):
    if arr is None:
        return np.zeros(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or q <= 0:
        return np.zeros(q, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.empty(q, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(q):
        a = (i * n) // q
        b = ((i + 1) * n) // q
        if b <= a:
            b = a + 1
            if b > n:
                b = n
                a = max(0, n - 1)
        out[i] = float(np.mean(arr[a:b])) if b > a else 0.0
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        out /= s
    else:
        out.fill(1.0 / q)
    return out


def _pattern_seq(theta, q, K):
    h = _softmax(theta)
    if h is None:
        return None
    if q <= 0 or K <= 0:
        return None
    return np.tile(h, int(K)) / float(K)


def _pattern_lse(theta, q, K, beta, top_count=256):
    p = _pattern_seq(theta, q, K)
    if p is None:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    b = _conv_full(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)

    z = beta * b
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    wsum = float(np.sum(w))
    if wsum <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(wsum):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
    val = float((zm + math.log(wsum)) / beta)

    tc = int(min(max(8, top_count), b.size))
    if tc < b.size:
        idx = np.argpartition(w, -tc)[-tc:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(b.size)
    wt = w[idx]
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, wval in zip(idx, wt):
        if wval <= 1e-15:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        pos = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
        src = k - pos
        d[pos] += 2.0 * (wval / wsum) * p[src]

    psum = float(d.sum())
    if psum == 0.0:
        return val, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
    h = _softmax(theta)
    try:
        G = d.reshape(int(K), int(q)).sum(axis=0)
        grad = (h * psum - G) / float(K)
    except Exception:
        grad = np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(grad).all():
        grad = np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    return val, grad


def _build_convolution_matrix(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for d in range(n):
        if d < n:
            A[d:2 * n - d - 1, :n - d] = a[d]
    return A


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    np.random.seed(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 12345)
    budget_s = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    def time_left():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    best = {"arr": None, "score": float("inf"), "fft": float("inf")}
    pool = []

    def add_pool(p):
        p = _normalize_sequence(p)
        if p is None:
            return
        if 64 <= p.size <= 1024:
            s = _score_fast(p)
            if math.isfinite(s) and s < 3.5:
                pool.append((s, p.copy()))

    def consider_array(p, s_fft=None):
        p = _normalize_sequence(p)
        if p is None:
            return
        if s_fft is None or not math.isfinite(s_fft):
            s_fft = _score_fast(p)
        if not math.isfinite(s_fft):
            return
        if see this? no, just if:
            if s_fft < best["fft"] - 1e-12 or best["arr"] is None:
                best["fft"] = s_fft
                best["arr"] = p.copy()
        if math.isfinite(s_fft) and (best["score"] == float("inf") or s_fft < best["score"] - 1e-7):
            if p.size <= 8000:
                try:
                    s_ex = _exact_score_arr(p)
                    if math.isfinite(s_ex) and s_ex < best["score"]:
                        best["score"] = s_ex
                        best["arr"] = p.copy()
                except Exception:
                    if s_fft < best["score"]:
                        best["score"] = s_fft
            else:
                if s_fft < best["score"]:
                    best["score"] = s_fft

    raw_parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent_p = _normalize_sequence(raw_parent) if raw_parent is not None else None

    # Always have a valid non-infinite fallback.
    if parent_p is not None:
        consider_array(parent_p, _exact_score_arr(parent_p))
    fallback_const = np.full(128, 1.0 / 128, dtype=np.float64)
    consider_array(fallback_const, _exact_score_arr(fallback_const))
    small_seed = np.asarray([0.2, 0.3, 0.2, 0.3], dtype=np.float64)
    consider_array(small_seed, _exact_score_arr(small_seed))

    if minimize is None:
        # Very small safe local polish only.
        if best["arr"] is not None and time_left() > 20:
            p = best["arr"]
            for _ in range(50):
                if time_left() < 5:
                    break
                c = p.copy()
                w = max(2, p.size // 32)
                s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, p.size - w + 1)))
                c[s:s + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2)))
                consider_array(c)
        if best["arr"] is None:
            best["arr"] = fallback_const
        return [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best["arr"], dtype=np.float64)]

    def pattern_starts(q):
        starts = []
        starts.append(np.zeros(q, dtype=np.float64))
        if parent_p is not None:
            seg = _segment_average(parent_p, q)
            th = np.log(np.clip(seg, 1e-8, None))
            th -= np.mean(th)
            starts.append(th)
        t = np.arange(q, dtype=np.float64) / float(q)
        for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
            for ph in (0.0, math.pi / 2):
                starts.append(0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph))
                starts.append(0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph))
        for _ in range(3):
            starts.append(0.7 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, q))
        # Limit starts.
        starts = starts[:10]
        return starts

    def consider_tiled(theta, q, max_N=4096):
        h = _softmax(theta)
        if h is None:
            return
        lengths = set()
        for mult in (2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128):
            N = int(q * mult)
            if 8 <= N <= max_N:
                lengths.add(N)
        if parent_p is not None:
            Np = int(parent_p.size)
            if Np >= max(8, 2 * q) and Np <= 6000:
                lengths.add(Np)
        for N in lengths:
            rep = max(1, (int(N) + int(q) - 1) // int(q))
            p = np.tile(h, rep)[:N]
            consider_array(p)
            add_pool(p)

    def pattern_optimize(q, K):
        N = int(q * K)
        if N < 2 or N > 5000:
            return
        beta = max(500.0, 5.0 * float(N))
        if N <= 64:
            top, maxiter, max_starts, itag = 16, 300, 8, 0
        elif N <= 512:
            top, maxiter, max_starts, itag = 64, 220, 6, 0
        elif N <= 2048:
            top, maxiter, max_starts, itag = 128, 140, 5, 0
        else:
            top, maxiter, max_starts, itag = 128, 70, 2, 0

        def fun_g(theta):
            if time_left() < 5.0:
                return 1e100, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
            val, g = _pattern_lse(theta, q, K, beta, top)
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.isfinite(g).all():
                g = np.zeros(int(q), dtype=np.float64)
            return float(val), g

        starts = pattern_starts(q)[:max_starts]
        for x0 in starts:
            if time_left() < max(60.0, 20.0 * (1 if N <= 512 else 3)):
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fun_g,
                    np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-10, "gtol": 1e-6},
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None
            for cand in (getattr(res, "x", None), x0):
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
                if cand.size != q:
                    continue
                p = _pattern_seq(cand, q, K)
                if p is not None:
                    consider_array(p)
                    add_pool(p)
                    consider_tiled(cand, q)

    # Small-resolution exact searches. These can find short strong sequences.
    small_specs = [(4, 1), (6, 1), (8, 1), (10, 1), (12, 1), (16, 1), (24, 1)]
    for q, K in small_specs:
        if time_left() < 120:
            break
        pattern_optimize(q, K)

    # Medium/large piecewise-constant patterns.
    medium_specs = [
        (8, 64),
        (16, 64),
        (16, 32),
        (32, 32),
        (64, 16),
        (32, 64),
        (64, 32),
    ]
    for q, K in medium_specs:
        N = q * K
        if time_left() < 180:
            break
        if N > 4096:
            continue
        pattern_optimize(q, K)

    # If we still have time, try one widest useful block.
    if time_left() > 350:
        pattern_optimize(64, 64)

    # LP active-set refinement on manageable-length non-uniform candidates.
    if linprog is not None and time_left() > 220 and pool:
        pool = sorted(set((round(s, 9), int(p.size)) for s, p in pool), key=lambda x: x[0])
        # Rebuild a deterministic pool sorted by score.
        pool2 = sorted(pool, key=lambda x: x[0])
        used = set()
        done = 0
        for _, p in sorted(((s, p) for s, p in pool), key=lambda x: x[0]):
            if time_left() < 60:
                break
            n = int(p.size)
            if n < 64 or n > 1024:
                continue
            key = (n, float(np.round(p, 12).sum()))
            if key in used:
                continue
            used.add(key)
            done += 1
            if done > 3:
                break

            def lp_refine(p0, total_allow):
                n = int(p0.size)
                S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
                a = _normalize_sequence(p0).copy() * S if _normalize_sequence(p0) is not None else None
                if a is None:
                    return p0
                best_p = a / S
                b = _conv_full(best_p)
                best_s = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b))) if b.size else 1e100
                M = 1.0001 * float(np.max(b)) if b.size and math.isfinite(float(np.max(b))) else best_s
                no_improve = 0
                start = time.monotonic()

                while time_left() > 45 and time.monotonic() - start < total_allow and no_improve < 7:
                    rem_lp = max(5.0, min(25.0, time_left() / 2.0))
                    improved = False
                    for tilt in range(2):
                        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                        if tilt:
                            c += 1e-4 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                        try:
                            A = _build_convolution_matrix(a)
                            res = linprog(
                                c,
                                A_ub=A,
                                b_ub=M,
                                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                                method="highs",
                                options={"time_limit": rem_lp, "presolve": True},
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            continue
                        if res is None or res.status not in (0, 3) or res.x is None:
                            continue
                        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0 * S)
                        L = float(g.sum())
                        if L <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        gnorm = g * (S / L)
                        base_p = a / S
                        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75):
                            pn = (1.0 - t) * base_p + t * gnorm
                            pn = np.clip(pn, 0.0, None)
                            ss = float(pn.sum())
                            if ss <= 0.0:
                                continue
                            pn = pn / ss
                            s2 = _score_fast(pn)
                            if math.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_s - 1e-12:
                                best_p = pn
                                best_s = s2
                                a = best_p * S
                                M = 1.0001 * best_s
                                improved = True
                                break
                        if improved:
                            no_improve = 0
                            break
                    if not improved:
                        no_improve += 1
                        pp = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
                        pp = _normalize_sequence(pp)
                        if pp is not None:
                            sp = _score_fast(pp)
                            if sp < best_s - 1e-12:
                                best_p = pp
                                best_s = sp
                                a = best_p * S
                                M = 1.0001 * best_s
                                no_improve = 0
                return best_p

            cand = lp_refine(p, max(20.0, min(120.0, time_left() * 0.35)))
            if cand is not None:
                consider_array(cand)
                add_pool(cand)

    # Final bounded local polish of current best (or a manageable alternative).
    search_p = None
    if best["arr"] is not None and 16 <= int(best["arr"].size) <= 4096:
        search_p = best["arr"]
    elif pool:
        search_p = min(pool, key=lambda x: x[0])[1]
    if search_p is not None and time_left() > 120:
        p = np.asarray(search_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        p = _normalize_sequence(p)
        if p is not None:
            n = int(p.size)
            b = _conv_full(p)
            cur = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b))) if b.size else 1e100
            step = 0.06
            stall = 0

            while time_left() > 30 and stall < 80:
                rmode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
                cand = None
                if rmode == 0:
                    th = np.log(np.clip(p, 1e-12, None)) + step * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                    cand = _softmax(th)
                elif rmode == 1:
                    cand = p.copy()
                    lo_w = max(2, n // 256)
                    hi_w = max(lo_w + 1, n // 16)
                    w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(math.log(lo_w), math.log(hi_w))))
                    w = min(w, max(2, n - 1))
                    s0 = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
                    cand[s0:s0 + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
                    if rng.random() < 0.25:
                        cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
                elif rmode == 2:
                    if b.size:
                        k = int(np.argmax(b))
                        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                        hi = min(n - 1, k)
                        pos = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                        src = k - pos
                        d[pos] = 2.0 * p[src]
                        d -= float(np.mean(d))
                        cand = p - 0.08 * d
                else:
                    if n > 8:
                        m1 = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(8, n // 4))))
                        m2 = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(8, n // 4))))
                        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
                        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                        mod = 1.0 + 0.12 * (np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m1 * t + ph1)
                                            + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m2 * t + ph2))
                        cand = p * mod
                if cand is None:
                    stall += 1
                    continue
                cand = _normalize_sequence(cand)
                if cand is None:
                    stall += 1
                    continue
                s2 = _score_fast(cand)
                if math.isfinite(s2) and s2 < cur - 1e-12:
                    p = cand
                    cur = s2
                    b = _conv_full(p)
                    step = min(step * 1.03, 0.45)
                    stall = 0
                    consider_array(p, cur)
                else:
                    step = max(step * 0.97, 0.015)
                    stall += 1

    # Final verification and guaranteed valid return.
    final_arr = best.get("arr")
    if final_arr is None:
        final_arr = fallback_const
    final_arr = np.asarray(final_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ssum = float(final_arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(ssum) or ssum < 0.01:
        final_arr = fallback_const
    else:
        final_arr = final_arr / ssum

    # The supplied evaluator is authoritative. If the exact score is bad, fallback.
    try:
        exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_arr])
    except Exception:
        exact = float("inf")
    if not math.isfinite(exact):
        final_arr = fallback_const
        final_arr = final_arr / float(final_arr.sum())
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_arr])
        except Exception:
            exact = float("inf")

    if not math.isfinite(exact):
        final_arr = np.full(128, 1.0 / 128, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
